I wouldn’t say I don’t care at all… like, I still do care about those things. That’s partly why it feels bad when I don’t keep up with them.

I’ve been trying to clean up my image and routine for some arts board stuff, and I’m still doing the basics, mostly, so it’s not like everything is falling apart or anything… but I keep dropping little things I usually care about, like fair planning notes or design drafts.\n\nThen I feel really guilty about it and end up canceling plans instead of just catching up. I’m not sure if that’s, like, serious enough to be here, but it’s been bothering me.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you’re not enjoying things as much as you usually do, even activities that normally make you happy?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure. I don’t think it’s that I can’t enjoy things at all… like, if I actually get myself there or start something, I can still feel okay. It’s more that I’ve been putting things off or not feeling as excited beforehand, if that makes sense. I don’t want to overstate it.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you’ve lost interest in things you normally enjoy, even after starting them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so… not once I’ve actually started. Like, I might drag my feet getting into something, but if I’m there doing it, I can usually still get some enjoyment out of it. I hope that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired or lacking energy lately, even after getting enough rest?\n\nPatient: Um… I don’t think I’ve noticed that exactly. I mean, I have days where I feel kind of worn out, but I wouldn’t say I’m tired even after resting or anything like that. At least, not in a way that really stands out. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, even if it's not completely overwhelming?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think a little, yes. I wouldn’t say it’s been completely overwhelming or that I’m not functioning, because I am still doing the basics. But since I started trying to clean up my image and routine for the arts board stuff, I’ve been more tense and down than I expected.\n\nIt’s more like… I keep dropping small things I normally care about, like fair planning notes or design drafts, and then I feel guilty about it and cancel plans instead of just catching up. So, yes, I guess I have been feeling down a lot of the day, but I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any trouble sleeping lately, like not being able to fall asleep, waking up too early, or sleeping much more than usual?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so, not in a major way. My sleep hasn’t really jumped out at me as a problem. I mean, maybe I’ve had a night here or there where it takes a bit to settle down, but I wouldn’t say I’m regularly waking up too early or sleeping a lot more than usual. I think it’s mostly been okay… if that answers it.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re letting others down or feeling badly about yourself more often lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess I have been feeling that more. Not like I think I’m a terrible person all the time or anything, but when I drop little things I normally care about — like the fair planning notes or design drafts — I feel really guilty afterward.\n\nThen instead of just catching up, I’ll cancel plans or avoid people because I feel like I’m letting them down. I know it probably sounds small, but it’s been happening enough that it’s been bothering me. I don’t think I’m speaking slower or moving slower than usual.\n\nRestless, maybe a little when I’m nervous, but I wouldn’t say it’s more often than not or something other people would notice. I hope that’s specific enough.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that once you start an activity, you feel like you’re just going through the motions and aren’t really getting anything out of it, even if you used to enjoy it?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I’d put it that strongly. Yeah… I mean, not like I truly think I’m a failure as a person, but I do get that feeling that I’m letting people down.

I haven’t really noticed that things feel completely empty or pointless after I’ve started them. I hope that answers what you’re asking.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you no longer care about things you used to care about, like your usual hobbies or responsibilities, even when you’re not busy or stressed?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say I don’t care at all… like, I still do care about those things. I’m not sure if that’s, like, serious enough to be here, but it’s been bothering me.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you’re not enjoying things as much as you usually do, even activities that normally make you happy?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure. I don’t think it’s that I can’t enjoy things at all… like, if I actually get myself there or start something, I can still feel okay. It’s more that I’ve been putting things off or not feeling as excited beforehand, if that makes sense. I don’t want to overstate it.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you’ve lost interest in things you normally enjoy, even after starting them?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so… not once I’ve actually started. Like, I might drag my feet getting into something, but if I’m there doing it, I can usually still get some enjoyment out of it. I hope that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired or lacking energy lately, even after getting enough rest?\n\nPatient: Um… I don’t think I’ve noticed that exactly. I mean, I have days where I feel kind of worn out, but I wouldn’t say I’m tired even after resting or anything like that. At least, not in a way that really stands out. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, even if it's not completely overwhelming?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think a little, yes. I wouldn’t say it’s been completely overwhelming or that I’m not functioning, because I am still doing the basics. But since I started trying to clean up my image and routine for the arts board stuff, I’ve been more tense and down than I expected.\n\nIt’s more like… I keep dropping small things I normally care about, like fair planning notes or design drafts, and then I feel guilty about it and cancel plans instead of just catching up. So, yes, I guess I have been feeling down a lot of the day, but I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any trouble sleeping lately, like not being able to fall asleep, waking up too early, or sleeping much more than usual?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so, not in a major way. My sleep hasn’t really jumped out at me as a problem. I mean, maybe I’ve had a night here or there where it takes a bit to settle down, but I wouldn’t say I’m regularly waking up too early or sleeping a lot more than usual. I think it’s mostly been okay… if that answers it.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re letting others down or feeling badly about yourself more often lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess I have been feeling that more. Not like I think I’m a terrible person all the time or anything, but when I drop little things I normally care about — like the fair planning notes or design drafts — I feel really guilty afterward.\n\nThen instead of just catching up, I’ll cancel plans or avoid people because I feel like I’m letting them down. I know it probably sounds small, but it’s been happening enough that it’s been bothering me. Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced a loss of interest or pleasure in activities, even after you've started them, not just before you begin them?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I’d call it a loss of pleasure, exactly. I mean… once I actually start something, I can usually get through it and sometimes enjoy parts of it.\n\nIt’s more that I hesitate or put it off beforehand, if that makes sense. I haven’t really noticed that things feel completely empty or pointless after I’ve started them. I hope that answers what you’re asking.\n\nDoctor: Have you found that you no longer care about things you used to care about, like your usual hobbies or responsibilities, even when you’re not busy or stressed?\n\nPatient: I wouldn’t say I don’t care at all… like, I still do care about those things. That’s partly why it feels bad when I don’t keep up with them.\n\nSince I started trying to clean up my image and routine for the arts board stuff, I’ve just been more tense and kind of down than I expected. I’m still doing the basic things, but I’ll drop smaller responsibilities I normally care about, like fair planning notes or design drafts. And then I feel guilty and sometimes cancel plans instead of just catching up.\n\nSo… it’s not that I don’t care, exactly. I hope that answers what you’re asking.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re a failure or that you’re letting people down in a way that feels excessive or unfair to yourself?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, not like I truly think I’m a failure as a person, but I do get that feeling that I’m letting people down.\n\nSince I tried to clean up my image and get my routine together for the arts board stuff, I’ve been more tense and kind of down than I expected. I’m not sure I’d say “loss of pleasure,” exactly. I mean, I still like the things I like once I’m doing them, usually.

Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed feeling down or depressed most of the day, nearly every day, even if it's not completely overwhelming?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think a little, yes. So, yes, I guess I have been feeling down a lot of the day, but I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any trouble sleeping lately, like not being able to fall asleep, waking up too early, or sleeping much more than usual?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so, not in a major way. My sleep hasn’t really jumped out at me as a problem. I mean, maybe I’ve had a night here or there where it takes a bit to settle down, but I wouldn’t say I’m regularly waking up too early or sleeping a lot more than usual.
